## 崇禎松江府志卷之三十四　選舉上（進士鄉舉）

<span style="margin-left: 3em">選舉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">呂東萊云取士科目不見於經然攷之魯論臯陶伊尹</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">非選舉之始乎吾松嚴助舉賢良文學陸康舉孝廉陸</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">績舉掾較去弓旌遠甚及</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">明仍隋唐進士科獨稱華選士以此自好莫敢跅𧿇敗</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">群而前輩亦相與成就愛惜之以待國家緩急之用即</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">天意亦若偏私偏重於科第諸賢而必不旁出於艸野</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">屠釣之內四科三立何其袞袞相望也夫　明主齋沐</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">焚香求之於禁中又錄其名姓扁藏於　天府焚告於</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">祖陵此與成周獻賢能之書於王王再拜而受之又加</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">重矣嘻是不獨爲松告也</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">兩漢　六朝</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">賢良文學</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">漢孝武建元元年詔丞相御史列侯中二千石諸侯相</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">舉賢良方正直言極諫之士　又詔舉賢良文學待以</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">不次之位於是會稽郡舉嚴助</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">漢嚴助<sub class="note-yuan">擢中大夫後爲會稽太守助吳人隱於華亭之嚴山</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">孝廉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">東漢百官志曰郡太守舉孝廉郡口二十萬舉一人又</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">郡國守相視事未滿歲不得察舉孝廉以其未久不周</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">知也其重如此故賢良一科奏對輒百餘人而孝廉之</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">選文帝詔云萬家之縣無應令者武帝詔云闔郡不薦</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">一人蓋孝廉非有實行可見者不容謬舉故耳</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">漢陸康<sub class="note-yuan">字季寧先經郡守莊旻舉孝廉至是李肅再舉</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">晉陸雲<sub class="note-yuan">字士龍年十六舉孝廉</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">薦辟</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">兩漢仕進之門或公府辟召或郡國薦舉或由曹掾積</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">累而升魏晉以來相沿不廢然漢制公府辟掾屬州郡</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">選曹皆自薦舉而自試用之苟非其人非特累衡鑑之</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">明抑且失倚毗之助</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">漢陸績<sub class="note-yuan">字公紀孫權辟爲奏曹掾</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸瑁<sub class="note-yuan">字子璋嘉禾元年公車徵拜議郎選曹尚書</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸褘<sub class="note-yuan">字元容赤烏六年徵爲宿衛郎中</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">晉張翰字季鷹<sub class="note-jiao">校：膺字當鷹字之訛今改]齊王冏辟爲大司馬東曹掾</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸機<sub class="note-yuan">字士衡張華薦之揚駿辟爲祭酒</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸曄<sub class="note-yuan">字士光元帝初鎮江左辟爲祭酒累遷散騎常侍本郡大中</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸納<sub class="note-yuan">字祖言初辟爲鎮軍大將軍武陵王掾</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">梁陸倕<sub class="note-yuan">字佐公年十七舉本州秀才刺史</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">唐</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">進士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">隋煬帝建進士科唐因其舊雖與秀才明經俊士並設</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">而進士特榮每一年一試所收不得過二十人開元前</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">未屬禮部以考功員外郎主之唐武后自詭文墨乃策</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">問於殿前後遂於省試外別有廷試</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">玄宗開元</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顧道行<sub class="note-yuan">字正平野王孫仕爲湖州司功參軍</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">肅宗至德二載</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顧况<sub class="note-yuan">字逋翁官著作佐郎</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">况負詩名以省試未得前列爲恨試題空梁落鷰</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">泥詩云卷幕差池燕常啣濁水泥爲黏朱履迹未</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">等畫梁齊舊點痕猶淺新巢緝尚低不緣頻上落</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">那得此飛棲</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">代宗大曆二年</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸贄<sub class="note-yuan">字敬輿西京試六人又中博學宏詞拔萃科仕至宰相</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">唐科試集載陸贄禁中春松詩　陰陰清禁裏蒼</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">翠滿春松雨露恩偏近陽和色更<sub class="note-yuan">一作正</sub>濃高枝分</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">曉日靈韻<sub class="note-yuan">一作虛吹</sub>雜宵鐘嵐助<sub class="note-yuan">一作香動</sub>爐烟遠形疑蓋</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">影重願符千歲壽不羡五株封儻得<sub class="note-yuan">一作長得</sub>迴天眷</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">全勝老碧峯西京試題</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">僖宗光啓二年</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸扆<sub class="note-yuan">字祥文禮第一人</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">北夢瑣言曰僖宗幸梁汴扆隨駕至行在爲宰相</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">韋昭度所知時已深夏以鄭損爲主司其年六月</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">狀頭及第後在翰林暑時苦熱同列戲之曰今日</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">好造榜矣</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">賜第</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">唐時有勅賜及第以表特恩者或文學之臣獻文別試</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">或正額之外績奏甚放及第皆稱賜第實一時之榮遇</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">也</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顧非熊<sub class="note-yuan">况之子會昌中陳商放榜上怪無非熊名召有司追榜勅使及第</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">明經</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">唐明經之別有五經有三經有二經有三禮有三傳此</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">歲舉之常永隆間以明經多抄義條亡裨<sub class="note-jiao">校：俾字當裨字之訛今改</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">實用自後試帖十得六以上又開元勅曰明經以帖括</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">爲功罕窮旨趣其問大義十條對時務策三首</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">丁公著<sub class="note-yuan">字平子五經及第又通開元禮授集賢校書郎</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">顧　謙<sub class="note-yuan">字自修舉明</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">經三禮二科</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">徵辟</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陸龜蒙<sub class="note-yuan">字魯望刺史張摶辟以自佐李尉盧携爲國召拜左拾遺</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宋</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">進士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宋設進士有一甲至五甲者有一舉至十五舉者雖同</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">五經學究明經而禮數輕重特異陳舜俞曰禮部貢院</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">試進士因唐故事設香案於堦前主司與舉人對拜所</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">坐設位供帳甚盛有司具茶湯飲漿至學究則悉徹帳</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">幕氈蓆茶漿之類渴飲硯水人皆黔其吻故當時有詩</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">云焚香禮進士徹幕待經生余應制科禮遇猶如此嘉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">祐後禮部試士無所謂五經學究而禮亦殺於前矣舜</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">俞華亭人</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">真宗天禧三年<sub class="note-yuan">王整榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">呂諤</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">仁宗天聖二年<sub class="note-yuan">宋郊榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">呂詢</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">景祐元年<sub class="note-yuan">張唐卿榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">呂評</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">慶曆六年<sub class="note-yuan">賈黯榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陳舜俞<sub class="note-yuan">嘉祐四年再應材識茂異賢良方正能直言極諫科擢第一</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宋時極重開封籍既易登第又得華選故借籍甚多吾</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">郡多開封貫以此鼂迥云舉人本貫難於取解奔輳京</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">轂寓籍充賦以致土著登名甚少交搆喧訟司馬光言</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">曰每次科第及第進士皆是國子監開封解送之人則</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">人之常情誰能舍此就彼哉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">嘉祐二年<sub class="note-yuan">章衡榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">呂全<sub class="note-yuan">用開</sub>　　　郟亶<sub class="note-yuan">字正夫江東轉運封貫</sub>　　　<sub class="note-yuan">判官遷太府丞</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">嘉祐六年<sub class="note-yuan">王俊民榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱伯虎<sub class="note-yuan">字才元用開封貫第五甲第二人朝奉大夫江東運判累贈太子少師</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">嘉祐八年<sub class="note-yuan">許將榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">戴顯甫<sub class="note-yuan">一姓錢</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱　鞏<sub class="note-yuan">字雄道伯虎從父</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">第三甲第二名</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">英宗治平二年<sub class="note-yuan">彭汝弼榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱伯熊<sub class="note-yuan">字才翁伯虎弟以朝散郎知興國軍轉朝奉大夫知萊州再任淮東提舉卒累贈宣奉大</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 5em">　<sub class="note-yuan">夫</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">董　乂<sub class="note-yuan">字彦臣江西貫魏王宫教授遞</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">遷大理寺卿封華亭開國男</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">神宗熙寧三年親試舉人初用策是日進士就席有司</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">猶循故事給禮部韻及題出乃策問也葉祖洽對言祖</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">宗多因循苟簡之政陛下即位聿而新之初考爲三等</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">覆考爲五等令宰相陳升之面讀以祖洽爲第一先是</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">吳公美名在甲榜竟以葉冠其前考官蘇軾疾其阿諛</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">因撰進士策一篇以進</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">熙寧三年<sub class="note-yuan">葉祖洽榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">吳公美<sub class="note-yuan">用開封貫</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">李　撰<sub class="note-yuan">彭澤令</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">字子約</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">熙寧六年<sub class="note-yuan">余中榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">呂　奎<sub class="note-yuan">用開</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 4em">　<sub class="note-yuan">封貫</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">元豐二年<sub class="note-yuan">時彦榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">柳廷俊<sub class="note-yuan">工部</sub>　　　　章粹<sub class="note-yuan">用建侍郎</sub>　　　　<sub class="note-yuan">寧貫</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">哲宗元祐三年<sub class="note-yuan">李常寧榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">呂益柔<sub class="note-yuan">字文剛甲科第二人用開封貫刑部侍郎以朝請大夫顯謨閣待制致仕</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">張徽言</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">元祐六年<sub class="note-yuan">馬涓榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱紱<sub class="note-yuan">伯虎從子甲科第二人後改名諤官尚書左丞謝恩詩云起家白屋連三榜平地青雲第二</sub>　　　　　富開　　　朱之純<sub class="note-yuan">用開名</sub>　　　　　　　　　<sub class="note-yuan">封貫</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">紹聖元年<sub class="note-yuan">畢漸榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">柳廷傑<sub class="note-yuan">廷俊</sub>　　　　呂佶</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 6em"><sub class="note-yuan">之弟</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">紹聖四年<sub class="note-yuan">何昌言榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">張天材<sub class="note-yuan">改名卿材</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">徽宗崇寧二年<sub class="note-yuan">霍端友榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">朱紘<sub class="note-yuan">紱弟第三甲第一人</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">崇寧五年<sub class="note-yuan">蔡薿榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">王箎　　　張甸　　　　呂少蒙</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">大觀三年<sub class="note-yuan">賈安宅榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">柳約<sub class="note-yuan">廷俊弟</sub>姚彀<sub class="note-yuan">改名</sub>　　張昭</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 5em"><sub class="note-yuan">焯</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">陳之元　　黃子服　　　衞上達</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">政和五年<sub class="note-yuan">何桌榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">黃鎮　　　黃鍰<sub class="note-yuan">鎮</sub>　　　衞開<sub class="note-yuan">八行科終</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 5em">　　　<sub class="note-yuan">弟</sub>　　　<sub class="note-yuan">宣教郎</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">張康朝</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">重和元年<sub class="note-yuan">王昻榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">衞闐<sub class="note-yuan">字致虛仲達從祖終朝奉大夫紹興倅贈太師魏國公</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">宣和元年<sub class="note-yuan">王俊又榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">衞膚敏<sub class="note-yuan">闐從子科第三人</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">宣和三年<sub class="note-yuan">何涣榜</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 3em">富說<sub class="note-yuan">開弟</sub></span>

<span style="margin-left: 3em">蔡廷秀<sub class="note-yuan">字君美由郡諸生試吏薦擢江西省檢校</sub></span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">國朝</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">選舉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">進士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">洪武四年辛亥舉會試壬子即罷科舉諭中書省曰朕</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">設科舉以求賢務得經明行修文質相稱之士以資任</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">用今有司所取多後生少年觀其文詞亦若可用及試</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">用之不能措諸行事朕以實心求賢而天下以虛文應</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">朕非朕責實求賢之意今各處科舉其暫停免令有司</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">察舉賢才凡罷進士十有二年</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">十七年甲子頒行科舉式凡三年一大比定於子午卯</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">酉年鄉試辰戌丑未年會試吾郡登進士科自乙丑始</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">洪武十八年乙丑科<sub class="note-yuan">丁顯榜</sub></span>

<span style="margin-left: 1em">永樂元年禮部奏壬午年兵革未舉鄉試請以今年八</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">月補之明年甲申會試禮部請取士之數　上問洪武</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">中所選幾何尚書李至剛對曰各科不同多者四百七</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">十餘人少者三十人　上曰朕即位姑率其多者不爲</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">例至剛華亭人</span>

<span style="margin-left: 3em">鄉舉</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">高皇帝洪武三年下詔曰朕聞成周之制取才於貢士</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">故賢者在職漢唐及宋科舉取士各有定制然但貴詞</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">章而不求德藝之實前元依古設科待士而權家勢要</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">結納奔競輒竊仕禄賢者耻于並進甘隱山林風俗之</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">敝一至於此今朕統一華夷願得賢人君子用之自今</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">八月爲始特設科舉以取懷才抱道之士務在經明行</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">修博通古今名實相稱其中選者朕將親策于廷第其</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">高下待以顯擢</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 1em">洪武三年庚戌科　是年以劉基秦裕伯爲考官未及</span>  
<span style="margin-left: 2em">會試悉授以官袁凱即授監察御史</span>
